### Sacred Cattle

| name | sacred\_cattle |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Sacred Cattle |

### Saharan Caravan Stop

| name | west\_african\_caravan\_stop\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Saharan Caravan Stop |
| description | Trade between sub-Saharan Africa and North Africa has been part of our identity for centuries. The journey across the vast desert, even with the many years of accumulated experience, is always taxing and dangerous. The key to securing trade in this region is the Caravan Stop, a place where merchants and their caravans can rest and refresh themselves on their way through the desert. |

### Salona's Stance

| name | sao\_salonas\_stance\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Salona's Stance |
| description | The mountain lords of Salona, perched around the ancient site of Delphi and controlling the strategic passes into central Greece, maintain a fierce independence through a combination of rugged terrain, stout fortifications, and a martial tradition that draws fighting men from across the Frankokratia. Even as larger states collapse, the lords of Salona repeatedly defy their enemies, turning their highland fief into a safe refuge of Latin knighthood in Greece and a persistent irritant to every power that seeks to dominate the region between the Corinthian Gulf and the Spercheios valley. |

| description | The Romagnol hills are dedicated to the cultivation of the Sangiovese grape. This is not just agriculture; it is a 'Warrior's Wine', robust and hearty. ### Santa Caterina's Legacy

| name | sie\_santa\_caterina |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Santa Caterina's Legacy |
| description | Caterina di Giacomo di Benincasa — mystic, theologian, and doctor of the faith — remains the most celebrated soul that Siena has ever produced, and in the age of religious upheaval that followed the great disputes of the era her memory became a rallying point for a confident, devotional faith that answered challenge with piety rather than polemic. Sienese scholars and preachers placed themselves at the forefront of this spiritual renewal, drawing on the saint's writings to argue that reform must begin in the heart before it reaches the law. This legacy of sanctity and conviction deepens the faith of our people and draws together communities that might otherwise fracture under the pressures of religious conflict. |
| icon | Advance church councils.png |

### Sanudo's Isles

| name | nax\_sanudos\_isles\_advance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Sanudo's Isles |
| description | Marco I Sanudo, a Venetian noble who participated in the Fourth Crusade, seized the Cyclades archipelago in 1207 and founded the Duchy of Naxos, creating a maritime feudal state whose lords ruled from fortified island citadels and drew income from trade, piracy suppression, and the fertile valleys of Naxos, Paros, and Santorini. By blending Venetian commercial instincts with Frankish feudal organization, the Sanudo dynasty has maintained a semi-independent duchy that serves as a vital link in the Latin trade network, protecting shipping lanes while extracting tolls and offering safe harbors to Crusader, Genoese, and Venetian vessels alike. |

| name | pea\_scuola\_del\_perugino |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | School of the Perugino |
| description | Pietro Vannucci — the *Perugino* — built the most admired workshop in Umbria within our walls, and his students fanned across Italy carrying the grace of the Umbrian style wherever commissions took them. Raphael came here to learn at his feet before Rome claimed the greater man, and the city that taught the teacher of Raphael carries an artistic reputation no rival in our region can easily dismiss. ### Seobe

| name | serbian\_seobe |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Seobe |
| description | The Serbian people have mastered the art of 'mobile identity'. This is characterized by the ability to move entire communities to new lands without losing cultural cohesion. This cultural mobility ensures that the 'Serbian Hearth' is not a fixed geographic point, but a portable and indestructible social contract; wherever the Serbian community strikes its staff, the old laws and the traditions of the *Slava* are immediately re-established. This resilience makes the Serbian culture functionally impossible to decapitate, as the nation can abandon scorched valleys only to reappear as a fully formed, military society on a new frontier, waiting for the opportune moment to strike back at the heart of their ancestral lands. |

### Sepak Takraw

| name | sepak\_takraw |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Sepak Takraw |
| description | Having been practiced all over South East Asia for a long time since its adaptation from the ancient Chinese *Cùjū*, and also known as *Sepak Raga*, *Takraw*, *Chinlone*, or *Sipa* depending on the country, *Sepak Takraw* is not just a game. Although the objective is to kick a ball between players and keep it in the air, it is also a way to have regular physical exercise, and thus played not only for entertainment but as military training for the army. |

### Separation of Powers

| name | separation\_of\_powers |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Separation of Powers |
| description | An incendiary, anonymous treatise on political theory has emerged. Despite efforts to suppress the text, it is spreading quickly across the world. It analyzes the nature of various forms of government, be they monarchies, despotisms, or democracies, and argues for a strict separation of the executive, legislative, and judiciary branches. This separation would protect the people and limit monarchical, which is understandably a very popular prospect for many of our subjects. Despite the unfortunate implications for the ruler's authority, these ideas may well benefit our nation greatly. |

### Serbian Enlightenment

| name | serbian\_enlightenment |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Serbian Enlightenment |
| description | Inspired by other nations of the Enlightenment, our national heroes, such as Dositej Obradović, have sought to elevate our vernacular language and literature. Touched by the dearth of secular works accessible to the common folk, Dositej Obradović has taken the lead in bridging the gap between written and spoken Shtokavian. By diligently crafting grammar books and dictionaries, authoring new tomes, and translating foreign works, our nation is fostering a literary revival thanks to the work of these visionaries. |

### Serbian Epic Poetry

| name | serbian\_serbian\_epic\_poetry |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Serbian Epic Poetry |
| description | Serbian people have developed a 'living history' through epic poetry. The *Guslar* — a wandering bard — is the primary guardian of the national soul. The *Guslar* recites thousands of verses accompanied by the *gusle*. These songs preserve genealogies, legal precedents, and moral imperatives. Serbian culture ensures that even the most illiterate shepherd has an understanding of ancestral rights and a shared identity. Every village gathering becomes a session of historical indoctrination. |

| name | ser\_serbian\_national\_militia |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Serbian National Militia |
| description | Drawing from the hard-won experience of our veteran units of the previous decades, we have established a permanent, professional standing National Militia. This force represents a decisive move away from the disorganized peasant levy, utilizing rigorous Western drill, uniform equipment, and a modernized chain of command while retaining the fierce, high-morale fighting style of our Balkan predecessors. This ensures that our independence is not a fragile diplomatic gift, but is instead backed by a credible, modern military machine that can defend our borders against any encroaching imperial power. |

| --- | --- |

| description | Our island trembles often, causing many buildings to collapse. In order to replace these old structures, the state will orchestrate one of the most ambitious rebuilding programs in human history. Rather than simply repairing old ruins, the government will redesign entire cities using the theatrical, earthquake-resistant *Sicilian Baroque* style. ### Sienese Civic Pride

| name | sie\_civic\_pride |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Sienese Civic Pride |
| description | Even as the grand ambitions of the Sienese republic gave way to foreign pressure and Tuscan consolidation, the citizens of Siena maintained a fierce pride in their distinct identity — their dialect, their art, their tradition of communal self-governance — that made the city difficult to absorb without provocation. Those who sought to antagonize or diminish Siena found its people stubborn in their resistance, bound together by centuries of shared memory and an insistence that Sienese life meant something worth defending. This deep-rooted civic spirit discourages aggression against us and sustains the prosperity that flows from a population genuinely invested in the wellbeing of the city they call home. |

### Sienese School of Painting

| name | sie\_artists |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Sienese School of Painting |
| description | The art of painting has flourished in Italy since the beginning of the Renaissance, but no artists can match the elegance of those trained in Siena, where the *Studium Senese* — one of the oldest universities in Europe — ensures that painters and scholars alike are nurtured by the same tradition of civic patronage. Sienese art is otherworldly and refined, with a heavy focus on the miracles performed by Biblical figures and Saints. This unity of learning and artistry defines the Sienese school, securing its reputation for centuries to come. |

### Social Banditry

| name | slo\_the\_social\_banditry |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Social Banditry |
| description | For decades, the cultural figure of the *Zbojník* (Highland Outlaw), epitomized by Juraj Jánošík, has become a military ideal in the minds of our people. This is the 'Culture of the Forest', where young men flee into the mountains to wage a guerrilla war against feudal oppression. While we have viewed them as bandits, the populace sees them as defenders. This cultural archetype has fostered a partisan mindset in the Slovak psyche: a distrust of central authority and a mastery of the scorched earth and mountain ambush tactics that can serve us well if we manage to nationalize it. |

### Spettacoli dell'Arena

| name | scaligers\_military\_ambitions |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Spettacoli dell'Arena |
| description | Verona's amphitheater is one of the finest surviving Roman monuments in Italy, and we have made it the stage for the greatest public *Spettacoli* in the north — bullfights, pageants, and civic competitions that draw crowds from Venice and the *Terraferma* for days at a time. No other city can offer this scale of spectacle inside ancient Roman stone, and the tradition has become inseparable from how Verona understands itself, a republic that still performs its identity before thousands of witnesses in a space built two thousand years ago. This reputation for public magnificence spreads our name across northern Italy and draws visitors whose spending and whose letters home do more for our standing than any envoy we could send. |
| icon | Advance monuments to greatness.png |

### Spoletine Silk

| name | spl\_the\_spoletine\_silk |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Spoletine Silk |
| description | The mulberry groves of our valley have fed silkworms that supplied the finest weavers in the Papal States for centuries, and the resulting *Spoletine silk* reached the altars of Rome and the wardrobes of courts as far as France and the Habsburgs. Our merchants built a prosperous burgher class on the back of this trade — one with enough surplus to educate its children and enough political sense to keep the guild networks running through every succession of papal governors. This luxury craft tradition fills our treasury from foreign demand and sustains the middling commercial class whose stability is the surest foundation of our peace. |

### Stato da Mar

| name | ven\_stato\_da\_mar |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Stato da Mar |
| description | From the days of the earliest *Doge*, Venice has built its power on the mastery of the sea — our merchants carry the riches of the East through the Adriatic while the *Stato da Mar*, our network of overseas ports and islands, secures the routes that sustain it all. At the heart of this maritime order stands the *Arsenale*, the greatest industrial complex of the medieval world, capable of launching a fully fitted galley in a single day when the Republic demands it. This combination of merchant dominance, state shipyard, and overseas empire makes Venice unlike any landlocked rival, transforming the sea itself into an extension of our state. |

### Statutes of Lithuania

| name | statutes\_of\_lithuania |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Statutes of Lithuania |
| description | Statutes of Lithuania are a set of three different legal reforms which aggregate, unify, and update the laws of the realm. Known for harsh punishments and the introduction of the death penalty, these statutes are in line with many contemporary Western penal codes. Each crime is supposed to be punished the same regardless of the class of the person committing it, but in practice, the peasants were often at a disadvantage compared to how members of other estates were treated. |

### Stories of the Gao Empire

| name | stories\_of\_the\_gao\_empire |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Stories of the Gao Empire |
| description | Before the Malian Empire came to dominate Western Africa, the city of Gao was the capital of an empire in its own right. These light cavalrymen, often wearing a mix of Byzantine armor and local attire, are masters of the 'hit and run' tactics necessary to harass larger, slower Western or Ottoman armies. They are the vanguard of a Greek military resurgence. |

### Studio Generale

| name | median\_studio\_generale |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Studio Generale |
| description | The university at Perugia, founded in 1308, placed itself under civic rather than ecclesiastical authority, and the schools that followed across our region trained the lawyers, notaries, and administrators who carried the business of the Italian commune forward. Our cities offered the libraries, the patronage, and the debating culture that drew men of learning from across the peninsula, making legal and philosophical training as natural a product of central Italy as any craft. This tradition of learned governance gives our officials a preparation in law and argument that their counterparts in more purely feudal regions struggle to match. |

### Susjedstvo of Bosnia

| name | bosnian\_susjedsvto\_of\_bosnia |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Susjedstvo of Bosnia |
| description | The ancient cultural concept of *Susjedstvo* — the sacred social obligation to protect and honor one's neighbor — has evolved into a sophisticated civic philosophy that transcends religious divides. In the face of external pressures, this unwritten code mandates that a neighbor's safety and property are as sacred as one's own. This practice prevents the 'Divide and Rule' tactics often used by foreign powers to incite internal strife. By prioritizing local solidarity and the 'Law of the Neighborhood', we maintain a level of social harmony and internal stability that allows the nation to present a single, unbreakable front against any attempt to dismantle our shared homeland. |
| icon | Advance government size.png |

### Swamp Drainage Projects

| name | svn\_swamp\_drainage\_projects |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Swamp Drainage Projects |
| description | By investing in large-scale hydraulic engineering projects to drain the vast marshlands of the riverine areas, we unlock thousands of acres of new, fertile farmland. These reclaimed lands will be soon settled, increasing the population density and tax base of the province while eliminating the vectors for disease. This transformation of the landscape not only strengthens our economy but also creates a more navigable and defensible terrain, removing the natural barriers that once hindered our own internal communications. |

### Swedish Copper Coins

| name | swedish\_copper\_coins |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Swedish Copper Coins |
| description | The abundance of copper in Sweden, primarily from the Stora Kopparberget mine in Falun, has prompted the country to use it as the main metal for minting a new national currency, the *daler*. This is issued as a large money plate, called *plåtmynt*, which can weigh up to 20 kilograms. Some argue that this might not be very practical and that the volume of money in circulation could exceed copper reserves, but they seem narrow-minded. |

| --- | --- |

| description | We do not need to construct a unique identity for our country, since our people readily rally around the banners of their cantons and the ideals of our confederacy. ### Swiss Tolerance

| name | swiss\_tolerance |
| --- | --- |
| display\_name | Swiss Tolerance |
| description | Why must we kill each other over obscure issues of transubstantiation and predestination, when we could spend that energy turning our mountain home into a realm united once more? For that reason, every Canton of Switzerland will have the ultimate jurisdiction over religious matters in their region. While this will not eliminate all religious strife in our country, it will at least ensure that no matter what someone believes, our people will be able to find a place where they are not persecuted. |
